```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Location Interface Contracts
 * Spatial topology graph, reciprocal portal meshes, and traversal routing.
 */

import type { SpatialPort, TopologyManifest } from './spatial-port.interface.js';

export type LightingLevel =
  | 'PITCH_BLACK'
  | 'FAINT_SEEPAGE'
  | 'INCANDESCENT'
  | 'BLINDING_GLARE';

export type PortalStatus = 'UNLOCKED' | 'LOCKED' | 'COLLAPSED';

export interface SpatialNodeDescriptor {
  readonly locationId: string;
  readonly displayName: string;
  readonly adjacentLocations: readonly string[];
  readonly acousticDampingBps: number;
  readonly lightingLevel: LightingLevel;
  readonly maxOccupants: number;
  readonly isCramped: boolean;
}

export interface PortalRecord {
  readonly portalId: string;
  readonly fromLocationId: string;
  readonly toLocationId: string;
  readonly status: PortalStatus;
  readonly clearanceRequirement?: string;
  readonly keyItemId?: string;
}

export interface TraversalRequest {
  readonly actorId: string;
  readonly fromLocationId: string;
  readonly toLocationId: string;
  readonly tick: number;
  readonly timestampMs: number;
}

export interface TraversalReceipt {
  readonly permitted: boolean;
  readonly actorId: string;
  readonly fromLocationId: string;
  readonly toLocationId: string;
  readonly portalId?: string;
  readonly leftEventId?: string;
  readonly enteredEventId?: string;
  readonly error?: string;
  readonly timestampMs: number;
}

export interface TopologyValidationReport {
  readonly valid: boolean;
  readonly totalLocations: number;
  readonly totalPortals: number;
  readonly originHubId: string;
  readonly isReciprocal: boolean;
  readonly isReachable: boolean;
  readonly asymmetricTraps: readonly string[];
  readonly orphanIslands: readonly string[];
  readonly selfAdjacencies: readonly string[];
  readonly timestampMs: number;
}

export default interface Location {
  idx: string;
  originHubId: string;
  locations: Map<string, SpatialNodeDescriptor>;
  portals: Map<string, PortalRecord>;
  occupants: Map<string, Set<string>>;
  spatialPort?: SpatialPort;
  topologyManifest?: TopologyManifest;
}
```

#### 4. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/fce/location.bit.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Location Bit Payload Contracts
 */
import type {
  SpatialNodeDescriptor,
  PortalRecord,
  PortalStatus,
  TraversalRequest,
  TraversalReceipt,
  TopologyValidationReport,
} from './location.interface.js';
import type {
  SpatialPort,
  TopologyManifest,
  PathReceipt,
  PerceptCandidate,
} from './spatial-port.interface.js';
import type { SpatialOverlaySnapshot, PackedAxial } from './spatial-overlay.interface.js';

export interface LocationPayload {
  readonly locationId?: string;
  readonly portalId?: string;
  readonly portalStatus?: PortalStatus;
  readonly node?: SpatialNodeDescriptor;
  readonly nodes?: readonly SpatialNodeDescriptor[];
  readonly portal?: PortalRecord;
  readonly portals?: readonly PortalRecord[];
  readonly traversalRequest?: TraversalRequest;
  readonly traversalReceipt?: TraversalReceipt;
  readonly topologyReport?: TopologyValidationReport;
  readonly occupants?: readonly string[];
  readonly timestampMs?: number;
  readonly error?: string;

  // SpatialPort binding and query payloads
  readonly spatialPort?: SpatialPort;
  readonly topologyManifest?: TopologyManifest;
  readonly overlaySnapshot?: SpatialOverlaySnapshot;
  readonly originPacked?: PackedAxial;
  readonly destinationPacked?: PackedAxial;
  readonly sourceCellPacked?: PackedAxial;
  readonly observerCellPacked?: PackedAxial;
  readonly emissionEnergyCdB?: number;
  readonly maxBudgetCostUnits?: number;
  readonly workEnvelopeLimit?: number;
  readonly pathReceipt?: PathReceipt;
  readonly perceptCandidate?: PerceptCandidate;
}

export default interface LocationBit<TDat = LocationPayload, TLst = string> {
  idx: string;
  src?: string;
  val?: number;
  dat?: TDat;
  lst?: TLst[];
  slv?: (result: { locBit: Partial<LocationBit<TDat, TLst>> }) => void;
}
```

#### 7. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/buz/location.buzz.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Location Buzz Business Logic
 * Reciprocal adjacency verification, portal crossing checks, and synchronous SpatialPort binding.
 */
import { LocationModel, BASELINE_TOPOLOGY_LOCATIONS } from '../location.model.js';
import type LocationBit from '../fce/location.bit.js';
import type {
  SpatialNodeDescriptor,
  PortalRecord,
  PortalStatus,
  TraversalReceipt,
  TopologyValidationReport,
} from '../fce/location.interface.js';
import type {
  PathReceipt,
  PerceptCandidate,
} from '../fce/spatial-port.interface.js';
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js';

const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console';

export const portalKey = (fromId: string, toId: string): string => `${fromId}->${toId}`;

const emitConsole = async (ste: State | undefined, src: string): Promise<void> => {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY;
  if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
    try {
      await lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src });
      return;
    } catch {
      // Degrade gracefully
    }
  }
  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    try {
      await ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src });
      return;
    } catch {
      // Degrade gracefully
    }
  }
};

export const initLocation = async (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LocationModel> => {
  cpy.locations.clear();
  cpy.portals.clear();
  cpy.occupants.clear();

  for (const node of BASELINE_TOPOLOGY_LOCATIONS) {
    cpy.locations.set(node.locationId, node);
    cpy.occupants.set(node.locationId, new Set<string>());
  }

  for (const node of BASELINE_TOPOLOGY_LOCATIONS) {
    for (const adjId of node.adjacentLocations) {
      const key = portalKey(node.locationId, adjId);
      if (!cpy.portals.has(key)) {
        cpy.portals.set(key, {
          portalId: key,
          fromLocationId: node.locationId,
          toLocationId: adjId,
          status: 'UNLOCKED',
        });
      }
    }
  }

  await emitConsole(ste, '>> [LOCATION:INIT] [OK] Baseline topology mesh synchronized.');
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'init-location-success', val: cpy.locations.size } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const updateLocation = async (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LocationModel> => {
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'update-location-pass' } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const registerLocation = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  const node = bal?.dat?.node;
  if (!node || !node.locationId) {
    if (bal?.slv) bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'register-location-fail', dat: { error: 'INVALID_NODE' } } });
    return cpy;
  }
  cpy.locations.set(node.locationId, node);
  if (!cpy.occupants.has(node.locationId)) {
    cpy.occupants.set(node.locationId, new Set<string>());
  }
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'register-location-success', dat: { node } } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const setPortalLockAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  const portalId = bal?.dat?.portalId;
  const status = bal?.dat?.portalStatus;
  if (!portalId || !status || !cpy.portals.has(portalId)) {
    if (bal?.slv) bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'set-portal-fail', dat: { error: 'PORTAL_NOT_FOUND' } } });
    return cpy;
  }
  const existing = cpy.portals.get(portalId)!;
  const updated: PortalRecord = { ...existing, status };
  cpy.portals.set(portalId, updated);
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'set-portal-success', dat: { portal: updated } } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const validateLocationAdjacency = (
  model: LocationModel,
  fromId: string,
  toId: string,
): boolean => {
  const node = model.locations.get(fromId);
  if (!node) return false;
  return node.adjacentLocations.includes(toId);
};

export const validateAdjacencyAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  const req = bal?.dat?.traversalRequest;
  if (!req) return cpy;
  const isAdjacent = validateLocationAdjacency(cpy, req.fromLocationId, req.toLocationId);
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'validate-adjacency-res', val: isAdjacent ? 1 : 0 } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const checkPortalCrossingAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  const req = bal?.dat?.traversalRequest;
  if (!req) return cpy;

  const isAdjacent = validateLocationAdjacency(cpy, req.fromLocationId, req.toLocationId);
  if (!isAdjacent) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'portal-crossing-fail',
          dat: {
            traversalReceipt: {
              permitted: false,
              actorId: req.actorId,
              fromLocationId: req.fromLocationId,
              toLocationId: req.toLocationId,
              error: 'PHYSICALLY_IMPOSSIBLE: Not adjacent',
              timestampMs: Date.now(),
            },
          },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const pKey = portalKey(req.fromLocationId, req.toLocationId);
  const portal = cpy.portals.get(pKey);
  if (portal && portal.status !== 'UNLOCKED') {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'portal-crossing-fail',
          dat: {
            traversalReceipt: {
              permitted: false,
              actorId: req.actorId,
              fromLocationId: req.fromLocationId,
              toLocationId: req.toLocationId,
              portalId: pKey,
              error: `SPATIAL_UNAVAILABLE: Portal is ${portal.status}`,
              timestampMs: Date.now(),
            },
          },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const destNode = cpy.locations.get(req.toLocationId);
  const currentOcc = cpy.occupants.get(req.toLocationId)?.size || 0;
  if (destNode && currentOcc >= destNode.maxOccupants) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'portal-crossing-fail',
          dat: {
            traversalReceipt: {
              permitted: false,
              actorId: req.actorId,
              fromLocationId: req.fromLocationId,
              toLocationId: req.toLocationId,
              error: 'CAPACITY_EXCEEDED: Destination is saturated',
              timestampMs: Date.now(),
            },
          },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      locBit: {
        idx: 'portal-crossing-success',
        dat: {
          traversalReceipt: {
            permitted: true,
            actorId: req.actorId,
            fromLocationId: req.fromLocationId,
            toLocationId: req.toLocationId,
            portalId: pKey,
            timestampMs: Date.now(),
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const traverseLocationAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  const req = bal?.dat?.traversalRequest;
  if (!req) return cpy;

  const fromSet = cpy.occupants.get(req.fromLocationId);
  const toSet = cpy.occupants.get(req.toLocationId);

  if (fromSet) fromSet.delete(req.actorId);
  if (toSet) toSet.add(req.actorId);

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      locBit: {
        idx: 'traverse-location-success',
        dat: {
          traversalReceipt: {
            permitted: true,
            actorId: req.actorId,
            fromLocationId: req.fromLocationId,
            toLocationId: req.toLocationId,
            leftEventId: `EVT_LEFT_${req.actorId}_${req.tick}`,
            enteredEventId: `EVT_ENTERED_${req.actorId}_${req.tick}`,
            timestampMs: Date.now(),
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const auditTopologySymmetry = (model: LocationModel): TopologyValidationReport => {
  let isReciprocal = true;
  const asymmetricTraps: string[] = [];
  const selfAdjacencies: string[] = [];

  for (const [locId, node] of model.locations.entries()) {
    if (node.adjacentLocations.includes(locId)) {
      selfAdjacencies.push(locId);
    }
    for (const adjId of node.adjacentLocations) {
      const neighbor = model.locations.get(adjId);
      if (!neighbor || !neighbor.adjacentLocations.includes(locId)) {
        isReciprocal = false;
        asymmetricTraps.push(`${locId}->${adjId}`);
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    valid: isReciprocal && selfAdjacencies.length === 0,
    totalLocations: model.locations.size,
    totalPortals: model.portals.size,
    originHubId: model.originHubId,
    isReciprocal,
    isReachable: true,
    asymmetricTraps: Object.freeze(asymmetricTraps),
    orphanIslands: Object.freeze([]),
    selfAdjacencies: Object.freeze(selfAdjacencies),
    timestampMs: Date.now(),
  };
};

export const auditTopologyAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  const report = auditTopologySymmetry(cpy);
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({ locBit: { idx: 'audit-topology-report', dat: { topologyReport: report } } });
  }
  return cpy;
};

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SYNCHRONOUS SPATIALPORT BINDING & EXECUTION BUZZERS
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const bindSpatialPortAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  const port = bal?.dat?.spatialPort;
  const manifest = bal?.dat?.topologyManifest;

  if (!port || typeof port.findPath !== 'function') {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'bind-spatial-port-fail',
          dat: { error: 'SPATIAL_PORT_INVALID: findPath function required' },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  cpy.spatialPort = port;
  if (manifest) {
    cpy.topologyManifest = manifest;
  }

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      locBit: {
        idx: 'bind-spatial-port-success',
        src: port.kernelVersion,
        dat: { spatialPort: port, topologyManifest: manifest },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const evaluateSpatialPathAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  if (!cpy.spatialPort) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'evaluate-spatial-path-fail',
          dat: { error: 'SPATIAL_UNAVAILABLE: SpatialPort is not bound' },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const manifest = bal?.dat?.topologyManifest || cpy.topologyManifest;
  const overlay = bal?.dat?.overlaySnapshot;
  const origin = bal?.dat?.originPacked;
  const destination = bal?.dat?.destinationPacked;
  const budget = bal?.dat?.maxBudgetCostUnits ?? 1000;
  const limit = bal?.dat?.workEnvelopeLimit ?? 512;

  if (!manifest || !overlay || origin == null || destination == null) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'evaluate-spatial-path-fail',
          dat: { error: 'INVALID_QUERY: manifest, overlay, origin, destination mandatory' },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  // Pure synchronous invocation inside local V8 isolate
  const receipt: PathReceipt = cpy.spatialPort.findPath(
    manifest,
    overlay,
    origin,
    destination,
    budget,
    limit,
  );

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      locBit: {
        idx: 'evaluate-spatial-path-success',
        val: receipt.ok ? 1 : 0,
        dat: { pathReceipt: receipt },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};

export const evaluateSpatialPerceptionAction = (
  cpy: LocationModel,
  bal?: LocationBit,
  ste?: State,
): LocationModel => {
  if (!cpy.spatialPort) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'evaluate-spatial-perception-fail',
          dat: { error: 'SPATIAL_UNAVAILABLE: SpatialPort is not bound' },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  const manifest = bal?.dat?.topologyManifest || cpy.topologyManifest;
  const overlay = bal?.dat?.overlaySnapshot;
  const sourceCell = bal?.dat?.sourceCellPacked;
  const observerCell = bal?.dat?.observerCellPacked;
  const energyCdB = bal?.dat?.emissionEnergyCdB ?? 7000;

  if (!manifest || !overlay || sourceCell == null || observerCell == null) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        locBit: {
          idx: 'evaluate-spatial-perception-fail',
          dat: { error: 'INVALID_QUERY: manifest, overlay, sourceCell, observerCell mandatory' },
        },
      });
    }
    return cpy;
  }

  // Pure synchronous invocation inside local V8 isolate
  const percept: PerceptCandidate = cpy.spatialPort.evaluatePerception(
    manifest,
    overlay,
    sourceCell,
    observerCell,
    energyCdB,
  );

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      locBit: {
        idx: 'evaluate-spatial-perception-success',
        val: percept.isOccluded ? 0 : 1,
        dat: { perceptCandidate: percept },
      },
    });
  }
  return cpy;
};
```

#### 9. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/location.reduce.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Location Reducer Router
 */
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './location.action.js';
import { LocationModel } from './location.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './location.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: LocationModel = new LocationModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State,
): LocationModel | Promise<LocationModel> {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.initLocation(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.UPDATE_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.updateLocation(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.REGISTER_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.registerLocation(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.SET_PORTAL_LOCK:
      return Buzz.setPortalLockAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.VALIDATE_ADJACENCY:
      return Buzz.validateAdjacencyAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.CHECK_PORTAL_CROSSING:
      return Buzz.checkPortalCrossingAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.TRAVERSE_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.traverseLocationAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.AUDIT_TOPOLOGY:
      return Buzz.auditTopologyAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.BIND_SPATIAL_PORT:
      return Buzz.bindSpatialPortAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.EVALUATE_SPATIAL_PATH:
      return Buzz.evaluateSpatialPathAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.EVALUATE_SPATIAL_PERCEPTION:
      return Buzz.evaluateSpatialPerceptionAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

#### 10. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/location.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { LocationModel } from './location.model.js';
import {
  initLocation,
  bindSpatialPortAction,
  evaluateSpatialPathAction,
  evaluateSpatialPerceptionAction,
} from './buz/location.buzz.js';
import type {
  SpatialPort,
  TopologyManifest,
  PathReceipt,
  PerceptCandidate,
  DisplacementResult,
} from './fce/spatial-port.interface.js';
import type { SpatialOverlaySnapshot } from './fce/spatial-overlay.interface.js';

describe('10.location.unit - Synchronous SpatialPort Client Package Binding Battery', () => {
  let model: LocationModel;

  const mockTopology: TopologyManifest = {
    topologyHash: 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855',
    bounds: { minQ: -10, maxQ: 10, minR: -10, maxR: 10 },
    blockedCells: new Set<number>(),
    portalEdges: new Map(),
    cellAcousticDampingCdB: new Map(),
    ambientNoiseFloorCdB: 3000,
  };

  const mockOverlay: SpatialOverlaySnapshot = {
    overlayHash: 'f4c2e11898fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b999',
    closedPortals: Object.freeze(['hatch_01']),
    impassableCells: Object.freeze([]),
  };

  const createMockSpatialPort = (version = '1.0.0'): SpatialPort => ({
    kernelVersion: version,
    findPath: (topology, overlay, origin, destination, maxBudgetCostUnits, workEnvelopeLimit): PathReceipt => {
      return {
        schemaVersion: 1,
        kernelVersion: version,
        topologyHash: topology.topologyHash,
        overlayHash: overlay.overlayHash,
        queryHash: 'q_path_123',
        receiptHash: 'r_path_456',
        ok: true,
        origin,
        destination,
        path: Object.freeze([origin, destination]),
        totalCostUnits: 150,
        cellsExpanded: 4,
      };
    },
    evaluatePerception: (topology, overlay, sourceCell, observerCell, emissionEnergyCdB): PerceptCandidate => {
      return {
        schemaVersion: 1,
        kernelVersion: version,
        topologyHash: topology.topologyHash,
        overlayHash: overlay.overlayHash,
        queryHash: 'q_perc_123',
        receiptHash: 'r_perc_456',
        channel: 'VISUAL',
        sourceCell,
        observerCell,
        signalCdB: emissionEnergyCdB,
        ambientNoiseCdB: topology.ambientNoiseFloorCdB,
        snrMarginCdB: emissionEnergyCdB - topology.ambientNoiseFloorCdB,
        bearingDegrees: 60,
        isOccluded: false,
      };
    },
    resolveDisplacement: (): DisplacementResult => {
      throw new Error('Not implemented');
    },
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    model = new LocationModel();
    await initLocation(model);
  });

  it('fails closed when evaluating path without a bound SpatialPort', () => {
    let response: any;
    evaluateSpatialPathAction(model, {
      idx: 'test-unbound',
      dat: {
        topologyManifest: mockTopology,
        overlaySnapshot: mockOverlay,
        originPacked: 100,
        destinationPacked: 200,
      },
      slv: (res) => {
        response = res.locBit.dat;
      },
    });

    expect(response).toBeDefined();
    expect(response.error).toContain('SPATIAL_UNAVAILABLE');
  });

  it('binds SpatialPort synchronously and verifies kernel version', () => {
    const port = createMockSpatialPort('4.2.0');
    let response: any;

    bindSpatialPortAction(model, {
      idx: 'bind-port',
      dat: { spatialPort: port, topologyManifest: mockTopology },
      slv: (res) => {
        response = res.locBit;
      },
    });

    expect(response.idx).toBe('bind-spatial-port-success');
    expect(response.src).toBe('4.2.0');
    expect(model.spatialPort).toBe(port);
    expect(model.topologyManifest).toBe(mockTopology);
  });

  it('executes findPath synchronously within host V8 isolate (zero Promises)', () => {
    const port = createMockSpatialPort('4.2.0');
    bindSpatialPortAction(model, {
      idx: 'bind-port',
      dat: { spatialPort: port, topologyManifest: mockTopology },
    });

    let pathResult: any;
    const actionReturn = evaluateSpatialPathAction(model, {
      idx: 'eval-path',
      dat: {
        overlaySnapshot: mockOverlay,
        originPacked: 101,
        destinationPacked: 102,
        maxBudgetCostUnits: 500,
      },
      slv: (res) => {
        pathResult = res.locBit.dat?.pathReceipt;
      },
    });

    // Purity assertion: must not be a Promise
    expect(actionReturn).not.toBeInstanceOf(Promise);
    expect(pathResult).toBeDefined();
    expect(pathResult.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(pathResult.totalCostUnits).toBe(150);
    expect(pathResult.kernelVersion).toBe('4.2.0');
  });

  it('executes evaluatePerception synchronously and computes exact SNR margin', () => {
    const port = createMockSpatialPort('4.2.0');
    bindSpatialPortAction(model, {
      idx: 'bind-port',
      dat: { spatialPort: port, topologyManifest: mockTopology },
    });

    let perceptResult: any;
    const actionReturn = evaluateSpatialPerceptionAction(model, {
      idx: 'eval-perc',
      dat: {
        overlaySnapshot: mockOverlay,
        sourceCellPacked: 101,
        observerCellPacked: 105,
        emissionEnergyCdB: 7500,
      },
      slv: (res) => {
        perceptResult = res.locBit.dat?.perceptCandidate;
      },
    });

    expect(actionReturn).not.toBeInstanceOf(Promise);
    expect(perceptResult).toBeDefined();
    expect(perceptResult.channel).toBe('VISUAL');
    expect(perceptResult.signalCdB).toBe(7500);
    expect(perceptResult.snrMarginCdB).toBe(4500); // 7500 - 3000
    expect(perceptResult.isOccluded).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

#### **User Story**

Elena sits in the dim glow of the terminal cockpit during the midnight shift of the production gauntlet. On the CRT monitor, two goblin scavengers—Bog and Skulk—creep through the sub-floor drainage tunnels toward the rusted siphon chamber. In previous iterations, the simulation had to trust that an ungrounded language model wouldn't hallucinate Bog spotting Skulk through three feet of solid concrete, or allow a goblin with a shattered tibia to sprint across eighty feet of mud in three seconds.

Now, with the synchronous SpatialPort bound directly into `10.location.unit`, Elena watches the execution logs snap across the console in microseconds. When Bog attempts to lunge, the simulation engine does not guess: it passes packed integer coordinates and a closed-portal bitmask across the airlock boundary into the pure geometry oracle. The spatial kernel runs an exact raycast and returns an unyielding receipt: occluded by closed blast hatch `hatch_valve_04`. The engine drops a hard brake on the narrative before a single word of script is drafted. Elena sips her lukewarm coffee, confident that every footstep, acoustic murmur, and sightline in the screenplay is physically locked by absolute planar geometry.

#### **Strategic Goal**

Supports the primary studio objective of **Sub-Millisecond Synchronous Spatial Feasibility at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$**. By binding the pure geometric `SpatialPort` client synchronously into `10.location.unit`, the simulation engine intercepts impossible spatial jumps, occluded line-of-sight assertions, and budget breaches in local host V8 RAM before allocating expensive neural speech synthesis, image diffusion, or relational database persistence.

#### **Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Establishes mathematical sovereignty over the physical universe of the story. It eliminates narrative drift and geographic hallucinations, guaranteeing that characters can only interact, observe, and traverse where the physical environment permits.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Enforcing a zero-direct-ingress epistemic boundary. The simulation engine holds rich, mutable character state (injuries, debts, grudges, identities), but the spatial oracle must remain completely stateless, pure, and ignorant of narrative lore. The port client must execute synchronously with zero Promise overhead, converting narrative state into packed axial integers and returning deep-frozen cryptographic proof envelopes in single-digit microseconds.

#### **Interface**

- **Target Repository:** `000.series-sower-engine` (`packages/300.engine`).
- **Interfacing Repository:** `000.goblin-spatial` (providing `@camp_candor/001.spatial` / `@goblin/spatial-types`).
- **Nature of Interaction:**
  - `000.series-sower-engine` imports exclusively type contracts and client signatures (`SpatialPort`, `TopologyManifest`, `SpatialOverlaySnapshot`, `PathReceipt`, `PerceptCandidate`).
  - Zero direct math algorithms or geometry kernels are copied into `300.engine`.

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Epistemic Sanitizer Interface Contracts
 * Strict plain-data contracts for scrubbed outbound spatial queries.
 */
import type { PackedAxial } from './spatial-overlay.interface.js';

export interface ScrubbedPathQuery {
```

#### 2. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/buz/axial-pack.buzz.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Integer Axial Coordinate Packing Transformer
 * Exact 32-bit unsigned integer packing adhering to Gillian Arithmetic Doctrine.
 */
import type { AxialCoord, PackedAxial } from '../fce/spatial-overlay.interface.js';

const AXIAL_OFFSET = 32768; // 16-bit signed range [-32768, 32767]

export const packAxial = (q: number, r: number): PackedAxial => {
  if (!Number.isInteger(q) || !Number.isInteger(r)) {
    throw new Error(`[AXIAL_PACK_ERROR] Coordinates must be discrete integers: q=${q}, r=${r}`);
  }

  const qShifted = (q + AXIAL_OFFSET) & 0xffff;
  const rShifted = (r + AXIAL_OFFSET) & 0xffff;

  return ((qShifted << 16) | rShifted) >>> 0;
};

export const unpackAxial = (packed: PackedAxial): AxialCoord => {
  if (!Number.isInteger(packed) || packed < 0 || packed > 0xffffffff) {
    throw new Error(`[AXIAL_UNPACK_ERROR] Invalid unsigned 32-bit packed coordinate: ${packed}`);
  }

  const qShifted = (packed >>> 16) & 0xffff;
  const rShifted = packed & 0xffff;

  const q = qShifted - AXIAL_OFFSET;
  const r = rShifted - AXIAL_OFFSET;
  const s = -q - r; // Cubic planar zero-sum invariant: q + r + s === 0

  return Object.freeze({ q, r, s });
};
```

#### 3. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/buz/sanitizer.buzz.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Outbound Epistemic Sanitizer Buzzer
 * Strips all character lore, actor identities, emotions, and narrative variables.
 */
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import type {
  ScrubbedPathQuery,
  ScrubbedPerceptionQuery,
  QuerySanitizationResult,
  RawSpatialQueryInput,
} from '../fce/sanitizer.interface.js';
import { packAxial } from './axial-pack.buzz.js';

const BANNED_NARRATIVE_PATTERNS = [
  /^char_/,
  /^actor_/,
  /^dialogue_/,
  /^trauma_/,
  /^grievance_/,
  /^emotion_/,
  /^wound_/,
  /name/i,
  /lore/i,
  /subtext/i,
  /speech/i,
  /belief/i,
];

const canonicalizeJson = (val: unknown): string => {
  if (val === null || typeof val !== 'object') {
    return JSON.stringify(val);
  }
  if (Array.isArray(val)) {
    return '[' + val.map((item) => canonicalizeJson(item)).join(',') + ']';
  }
  const keys = Object.keys(val as Record<string, unknown>).sort((a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
  return '{' + keys.map((k) => JSON.stringify(k) + ':' + canonicalizeJson((val as any)[k])).join(',') + '}';
};

const detectForbiddenNarrativeTokens = (obj: Record<string, unknown>): string[] => {
  const violations: string[] = [];

  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(obj)) {
    for (const pattern of BANNED_NARRATIVE_PATTERNS) {
      if (pattern.test(key)) {
        violations.push(`key:${key}`);
      }
      if (typeof value === 'string' && pattern.test(value)) {
        violations.push(`val:${key}=${value}`);
      }
    }
  }

  return violations;
};

export const sanitizePathQuery = (
  rawInput: unknown,
): QuerySanitizationResult<ScrubbedPathQuery> => {
  if (!rawInput || typeof rawInput !== 'object' || Array.isArray(rawInput)) {
    return { ok: false, strippedKeys: [], error: 'QUERY_PAYLOAD_MUST_BE_OBJECT' };
  }

  const record = rawInput as RawSpatialQueryInput;
  const breaches = detectForbiddenNarrativeTokens(record);

  if (breaches.length > 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      strippedKeys: [],
      error: `EPISTEMIC_FIREWALL_BREACH: Forbidden narrative tokens detected: [${breaches.join(', ')}]`,
    };
  }

  const strippedKeys: string[] = [];
  for (const k of Object.keys(record)) {
    if (!['qOrigin', 'rOrigin', 'qDest', 'rDest', 'budget', 'workLimit'].includes(k)) {
      strippedKeys.push(k);
    }
  }

  if (
    record.qOrigin == null ||
    record.rOrigin == null ||
    record.qDest == null ||
    record.rDest == null
  ) {
    return { ok: false, strippedKeys, error: 'MISSING_MANDATORY_COORDINATES' };
  }

  try {
    const originPacked = packAxial(Number(record.qOrigin), Number(record.rOrigin));
    const destinationPacked = packAxial(Number(record.qDest), Number(record.rDest));
    const maxBudgetCostUnits = Number.isInteger(record.budget) ? Number(record.budget) : 1000;
    const workEnvelopeLimit = Number.isInteger(record.workLimit) ? Number(record.workLimit) : 512;

    const scrubbedPayload: ScrubbedPathQuery = Object.freeze({
      originPacked,
      destinationPacked,
      maxBudgetCostUnits,
      workEnvelopeLimit,
    });

    const canonicalJson = canonicalizeJson(scrubbedPayload);
    const queryHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(canonicalJson).digest('hex');

    return {
      ok: true,
      scrubbedPayload,
      queryHash,
      strippedKeys: Object.freeze(strippedKeys),
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    return { ok: false, strippedKeys, error: err.message || 'AXIAL_PACK_FAILED' };
  }
};

export const sanitizePerceptionQuery = (
  rawInput: unknown,
): QuerySanitizationResult<ScrubbedPerceptionQuery> => {
  if (!rawInput || typeof rawInput !== 'object' || Array.isArray(rawInput)) {
    return { ok: false, strippedKeys: [], error: 'QUERY_PAYLOAD_MUST_BE_OBJECT' };
  }

  const record = rawInput as RawSpatialQueryInput;
  const breaches = detectForbiddenNarrativeTokens(record);

  if (breaches.length > 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      strippedKeys: [],
      error: `EPISTEMIC_FIREWALL_BREACH: Forbidden narrative tokens detected: [${breaches.join(', ')}]`,
    };
  }

  const strippedKeys: string[] = [];
  for (const k of Object.keys(record)) {
    if (!['qSource', 'rSource', 'qObserver', 'rObserver', 'energyCdB'].includes(k)) {
      strippedKeys.push(k);
    }
  }

  if (
    record.qSource == null ||
    record.rSource == null ||
    record.qObserver == null ||
    record.rObserver == null
  ) {
    return { ok: false, strippedKeys, error: 'MISSING_MANDATORY_COORDINATES' };
  }

  try {
    const sourceCellPacked = packAxial(Number(record.qSource), Number(record.rSource));
    const observerCellPacked = packAxial(Number(record.qObserver), Number(record.rObserver));
    const emissionEnergyCdB = Number.isInteger(record.energyCdB) ? Number(record.energyCdB) : 7000;

    const scrubbedPayload: ScrubbedPerceptionQuery = Object.freeze({
      sourceCellPacked,
      observerCellPacked,
      emissionEnergyCdB,
    });

    const canonicalJson = canonicalizeJson(scrubbedPayload);
    const queryHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(canonicalJson).digest('hex');

    return {
      ok: true,
      scrubbedPayload,
      queryHash,
      strippedKeys: Object.freeze(strippedKeys),
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    return { ok: false, strippedKeys, error: err.message || 'AXIAL_PACK_FAILED' };
  }
};
```

#### 4. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/buz/overlay-builder.buzz.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Dynamic Spatial Overlay Snapshot Builder
 * Synthesizes room portal occlusions and ephemeral cell obstacles into a deep-frozen snapshot.
 */
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import type { PortalRecord } from '../fce/location.interface.js';
import type { PackedAxial, SpatialOverlaySnapshot } from '../fce/spatial-overlay.interface.js';

const canonicalizeJson = (val: unknown): string => {
  if (val === null || typeof val !== 'object') {
    return JSON.stringify(val);
  }
  if (Array.isArray(val)) {
    return '[' + val.map((item) => canonicalizeJson(item)).join(',') + ']';
  }
  const keys = Object.keys(val as Record<string, unknown>).sort((a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
  return '{' + keys.map((k) => JSON.stringify(k) + ':' + canonicalizeJson((val as any)[k])).join(',') + '}';
};

export const buildSpatialOverlaySnapshot = (
  portals: ReadonlyMap<string, PortalRecord>,
  dynamicObstacles: readonly PackedAxial[] = [],
  costOverrides?: Readonly<Record<number, number>>,
  acousticOverridesCdB?: Readonly<Record<string, number>>,
): SpatialOverlaySnapshot => {
  const closedPortals: string[] = [];

  for (const [portalId, portal] of portals.entries()) {
    if (portal.status === 'LOCKED' || portal.status === 'COLLAPSED') {
      closedPortals.push(portalId);
    }
  }

  closedPortals.sort((a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
  const sortedObstacles = [...dynamicObstacles].sort((a, b) => a - b);

  const preImage = {
    acousticDampingOverridesCdB: acousticOverridesCdB || {},
    closedPortals,
    impassableCells: sortedObstacles,
    traversalCostOverrides: costOverrides || {},
  };

  const canonical = canonicalizeJson(preImage);
  const overlayHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(canonical).digest('hex');

  return Object.freeze({
    overlayHash,
    closedPortals: Object.freeze(closedPortals),
    impassableCells: Object.freeze(sortedObstacles),
    traversalCostOverrides: costOverrides ? Object.freeze({ ...costOverrides }) : undefined,
    acousticDampingOverridesCdB: acousticOverridesCdB ? Object.freeze({ ...acousticOverridesCdB }) : undefined,
  });
};
```

#### 7. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/location.reduce.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Location Reducer Router
 */
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './location.action.js';
import { LocationModel } from './location.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './location.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: LocationModel = new LocationModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State,
): LocationModel | Promise<LocationModel> {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.initLocation(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.UPDATE_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.updateLocation(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.REGISTER_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.registerLocation(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.SET_PORTAL_LOCK:
      return Buzz.setPortalLockAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.VALIDATE_ADJACENCY:
      return Buzz.validateAdjacencyAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.CHECK_PORTAL_CROSSING:
      return Buzz.checkPortalCrossingAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.TRAVERSE_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.traverseLocationAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.AUDIT_TOPOLOGY:
      return Buzz.auditTopologyAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.BIND_SPATIAL_PORT:
      return Buzz.bindSpatialPortAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.EVALUATE_SPATIAL_PATH:
      return Buzz.evaluateSpatialPathAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.EVALUATE_SPATIAL_PERCEPTION:
      return Buzz.evaluateSpatialPerceptionAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.SANITIZE_PATH_QUERY: {
      const result = Buzz.sanitizePathQuery((act.bale?.dat as any)?.rawQuery);
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          locBit: {
            idx: result.ok ? 'sanitize-path-success' : 'sanitize-path-fail',
            val: result.ok ? 1 : 0,
            dat: { error: result.error, pathReceipt: result as any },
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    case Act.SANITIZE_PERCEPTION_QUERY: {
      const result = Buzz.sanitizePerceptionQuery((act.bale?.dat as any)?.rawQuery);
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          locBit: {
            idx: result.ok ? 'sanitize-perception-success' : 'sanitize-perception-fail',
            val: result.ok ? 1 : 0,
            dat: { error: result.error, perceptCandidate: result as any },
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    case Act.BUILD_SPATIAL_OVERLAY: {
      const snapshot = Buzz.buildSpatialOverlaySnapshot(
        model.portals,
        (act.bale?.dat as any)?.dynamicObstacles || [],
        (act.bale?.dat as any)?.costOverrides,
        (act.bale?.dat as any)?.acousticOverrides,
      );
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          locBit: {
            idx: 'build-spatial-overlay-success',
            src: snapshot.overlayHash,
            dat: { overlaySnapshot: snapshot },
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

#### 8. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/sanitizer.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { packAxial, unpackAxial } from './buz/axial-pack.buzz.js';
import { sanitizePathQuery, sanitizePerceptionQuery } from './buz/sanitizer.buzz.js';
import { buildSpatialOverlaySnapshot } from './buz/overlay-builder.buzz.js';
import type { PortalRecord } from './fce/location.interface.js';

describe('10.location.unit - Outbound Epistemic Sanitizer & Coordinate Packing Battery', () => {
  it('encodes and decodes axial integer coordinates with zero error', () => {
    const coords = [
      { q: 0, r: 0 },
      { q: 12, r: -4 },
      { q: -55, r: 23 },
      { q: 32000, r: -32000 },
    ];

    for (const c of coords) {
      const packed = packAxial(c.q, c.r);
      expect(Number.isInteger(packed)).toBe(true);
      expect(packed).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);

      const unpacked = unpackAxial(packed);
      expect(unpacked.q).toBe(c.q);
      expect(unpacked.r).toBe(c.r);
      expect(unpacked.q + unpacked.r + unpacked.s).toBe(0); // Cubic zero-sum invariant
    }
  });

  it('rejects non-integer coordinates according to Gillian Arithmetic', () => {
    expect(() => packAxial(1.5, 0)).toThrow('discrete integers');
    expect(() => packAxial(0, 3.14159)).toThrow('discrete integers');
  });

  it('sanitizes valid path queries into scrubbed DTO with deterministic SHA-256 hash', () => {
    const raw = {
      qOrigin: 5,
      rOrigin: -2,
      qDest: 8,
      rDest: -5,
      budget: 450,
      workLimit: 256,
      extraneousMeta: 'should_be_stripped',
    };

    const result = sanitizePathQuery(raw);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(result.scrubbedPayload).toBeDefined();
    expect(result.scrubbedPayload?.maxBudgetCostUnits).toBe(450);
    expect(result.scrubbedPayload?.workEnvelopeLimit).toBe(256);
    expect(result.queryHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
    expect(result.strippedKeys).toContain('extraneousMeta');
  });

  it('fails closed when forbidden narrative tokens cross the boundary', () => {
    const dirtyQuery = {
      qOrigin: 0,
      rOrigin: 0,
      qDest: 2,
      rDest: -2,
      char_actor_id: 'char_bog',
      dialogue_line: 'We must cross before dawn',
    };

    const result = sanitizePathQuery(dirtyQuery);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(result.error).toContain('EPISTEMIC_FIREWALL_BREACH');
    expect(result.error).toContain('char_bog');
  });

  it('sanitizes perception queries and validates mandatory inputs', () => {
    const raw = {
      qSource: 1,
      rSource: 1,
      qObserver: 4,
      rObserver: -2,
      energyCdB: 6500,
    };

    const result = sanitizePerceptionQuery(raw);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(result.scrubbedPayload?.emissionEnergyCdB).toBe(6500);
    expect(result.queryHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  });

  it('builds deep-frozen spatial overlay snapshot with deterministic hash', () => {
    const portals = new Map<string, PortalRecord>();
    portals.set('door_a->door_b', {
      portalId: 'door_a->door_b',
      fromLocationId: 'loc_a',
      toLocationId: 'loc_b',
      status: 'LOCKED',
    });
    portals.set('vent_a->vent_b', {
      portalId: 'vent_a->vent_b',
      fromLocationId: 'loc_a',
      toLocationId: 'loc_vent',
      status: 'UNLOCKED',
    });

    const packedObstacle = packAxial(1, -1);
    const snapshot = buildSpatialOverlaySnapshot(portals, [packedObstacle]);

    expect(snapshot.overlayHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
    expect(snapshot.closedPortals).toEqual(['door_a->door_b']);
    expect(snapshot.impassableCells).toEqual([packedObstacle]);
    expect(Object.isFrozen(snapshot)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

**User Story** Orson sat back from the glowing phosphors of the terminal deck as the simulated sub-floor drainage tunnels of Wiregrass Basin surged forward through tick 480. In the narrative ledger, Bog and Skulk were locked in a mortal standoff over a cracked copper siphon valve, their shared resentment clock simmering at 9,400 basis points. But as Bog wheeled through the dark to claim the valve, Orson had to know that the pure spatial engine calculating the path would never become an accidental leak of dramatic truth.

Under the old architecture, passing a pathfinding request meant passing character IDs, active inventories, and emotional flags into external math routines, inadvertently coupling the story's soul to a geometric solver. With the Outbound Query Payload Epistemic Sanitization Filter humming inside `10.location.unit`, Orson watched the trace stream into `cns00`. The instant the simulation core prepared a raycast, the sanitizer descended like a razor. Character names vanished. Grievances and trauma tags dissolved. The raw prompt was stripped down to cold, unfeeling coordinate integers packed into unsigned 32-bit registers and an immutable hash of closed steel valves. The spatial oracle was blind to who Bog was, what he felt, or why he was running. It computed pure feasibility in four microseconds and tossed back a mathematical receipt. Orson smiled: the wall between physical reality and narrative conscience held absolute.

**Strategic Goal** Supports the primary architecture initiative of **Absolute Epistemic Isolation at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$**. By intercepting and stripping all character identities, emotional descriptors, dialogue, and lore from outbound queries before they reach `@camp_candor/001.spatial` (`000.goblin-spatial`), the engine prevents narrative contamination of the spatial oracle and enforces instant fail-closed halts on accidental lore leaks before any billable computation occurs.

**Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Guarantees that spatial, acoustic, and line-of-sight calculations remain completely agnostic of narrative stakes. A spatial oracle must evaluate whether a physical ray pierces an aperture or whether two points are reachable within an energy budget; it must never possess character knowledge, preserving the clean separation between Axis A (World Law) and Axis B (Physical Projection).
- **The Technical Challenge:** Constructing a zero-alloc, sub-microsecond sanitization and bit-packing pipeline in host RAM that aggressively traps forbidden narrative tokens (such as `char_*`, `actor_*`, `dialogue_*`, `trauma_*`) while losslessly transforming floating-point hazards into Gillian-pure 32-bit integer axial coordinates ($q + r + s = 0$) and cryptographically sealed RFC 8785 JSON digests.

**Interface**

- **Target Repository:** `000.series-sower-engine` (`packages/300.engine/10.location.unit`).
- **Interfacing Repository:** `000.goblin-spatial` (via `@camp_candor/001.spatial` / `@goblin/spatial-types`).
- **Nature of Interaction:**
  - `10.location.unit` ingests raw simulation intentions from `01.actor.unit` and `03.entity.unit`.
  - The sanitizer buzzers cleanse these payloads of all narrative strings and pack axial coordinates $(q, r)$ into unsigned 32-bit integers (`PackedAxial`).
  - The resulting `ScrubbedPathQuery` and `SpatialOverlaySnapshot` are deep-frozen and forwarded across the synchronous `SpatialPort` interface to `000.goblin-spatial`.
  - Returned plain receipts (`PathReceipt`, `PerceptCandidate`) contain only geometric feasibility codes, step costs, and cryptographic proof hashes.

**Interaction**

- **Terminal Cockpit Inspection:**
  1. The operator boots the Blessed terminal cockpit via `npm run dev` and selects `SIMULATION ENGINE`.
  2. Once the engine is in `READY` status, the operator navigates the runtime dispatch switchboard to `[17] SPATIAL FEASIBILITY PROBE` (or advances ticks with collocated entities).
  3. The operator triggers a spatial path or perception query with diagnostic payloads.
- **Telemetry Verification:**
  - Console pane `cns00` (columns 4–12) prints real-time 7-bit ASCII receipts verifying epistemic scrubbing and integer packing:
    ```plaintext
    >> [EPISTEMIC:SCRUB] Outbound query sanitized: 3 narrative keys stripped.
    ```

```typescript
  readonly fatigueBps: number; // Integer basis points [0, 10000]
  readonly painBps: number; // Integer basis points [0, 10000]
  readonly refusalStrainBps: number; // Integer basis points [0, 10000]
  readonly activeTrauma: readonly string[];
}

export interface SomaticBudgetReceipt {
  readonly entityId: string;
  readonly movementBudgetCostUnits: number; // Discrete integer traversal units (centiseconds)
  readonly bannedKineticVerbs: readonly string[];
  readonly canActuateTwoHandedVerbs: boolean;
  readonly canSprint: boolean;
}

export interface HeldItemDescriptor {
  readonly itemId: string;
  readonly occupiesHands: number; // Integer [0, 2]
}

export interface HandLoadEvaluation {
  readonly valid: boolean;
  readonly totalOccupiedHands: number;
  readonly availableArms: number;
  readonly error?: string;
}
```

#### 2. `packages/300.engine/03.entity.unit/buz/somatic-query.buzz.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 03.entity.unit - Somatic State Query Extractor
 * Extracts and asserts Gillian integer basis-point bounds from raw entity state.
 */
import type { SomaticState, LocomotionTier } from '../fce/somatic-budget.interface.js';

const VALID_TIERS: readonly LocomotionTier[] = Object.freeze([
  'UNRESTRICTED',
  'LIMP',
  'NEUROPATHY_SHUFFLE',
  'IMMOBILIZED',
]);

const assertInteger = (val: unknown, fieldName: string): number => {
  if (typeof val !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(val)) {
    throw new Error(`[SOMATIC_INTEGER_BREACH] ${fieldName} must be a discrete integer. Received: ${val}`);
  }
  return val;
};

const assertBasisPoint = (val: unknown, fieldName: string): number => {
  const intVal = assertInteger(val, fieldName);
  if (intVal < 0 || intVal > 10000) {
    throw new Error(`[GILLIAN_BPS_BREACH] ${fieldName} must be within [0, 10000] bps. Received: ${intVal}`);
  }
  return intVal;
};

export const extractSomaticState = (raw: any): SomaticState => {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') {
    throw new Error('[SOMATIC_QUERY_ERROR] Entity record must be an object');
  }

  const entityId = typeof raw.entityId === 'string' ? raw.entityId : 'char_unknown';
  const availableArms = Math.min(2, Math.max(0, assertInteger(raw.availableArms ?? 2, 'availableArms')));
  const availableLegs = Math.min(2, Math.max(0, assertInteger(raw.availableLegs ?? 2, 'availableLegs')));

  let locomotionTier: LocomotionTier = 'UNRESTRICTED';
  if (typeof raw.locomotionTier === 'string' && VALID_TIERS.includes(raw.locomotionTier as LocomotionTier)) {
    locomotionTier = raw.locomotionTier as LocomotionTier;
  } else if (availableLegs < 1) {
    locomotionTier = 'IMMOBILIZED';
  } else if (availableLegs < 2) {
    locomotionTier = 'LIMP';
  }

  const fatigueBps = assertBasisPoint(raw.fatigueBps ?? 0, 'fatigueBps');
  const painBps = assertBasisPoint(raw.painBps ?? 0, 'painBps');
  const refusalStrainBps = assertBasisPoint(raw.refusalStrainBps ?? 0, 'refusalStrainBps');

  const activeTrauma: string[] = [];
  if (Array.isArray(raw.activeTrauma)) {
    for (const t of raw.activeTrauma) {
      if (typeof t === 'string' && t.trim().length > 0) {
        activeTrauma.push(t.trim().toLowerCase());
      }
    }
  }

  return Object.freeze({
    entityId,
    availableArms,
    availableLegs,
    locomotionTier,
    fatigueBps,
    painBps,
    refusalStrainBps,
    activeTrauma: Object.freeze(activeTrauma.sort()),
  });
};
```

#### 3. `packages/300.engine/03.entity.unit/buz/budget-calculator.buzz.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 03.entity.unit - Biological Condition Budget Calculator
 * Derives scalar movement costs, banned kinetic verb masks, and hand capacities.
 */
import type {
  SomaticState,
  SomaticBudgetReceipt,
  HeldItemDescriptor,
  HandLoadEvaluation,
} from '../fce/somatic-budget.interface.js';

export const BASE_MOVEMENT_UNIT_COST = 100; // Integer centisecond base cost

export const deriveSomaticBudget = (somatic: SomaticState): SomaticBudgetReceipt => {
  const banned = new Set<string>();

  // 1. Arm Capability Analysis
  if (somatic.availableArms < 2) {
    banned.add('WIELD_TWO_HANDED');
    banned.add('CLIMB_ROPE');
    banned.add('DUAL_WIELD');
    banned.add('VAULT_OBSTACLE');
  }

  if (somatic.availableArms < 1) {
    banned.add('WIELD_ONE_HANDED');
    banned.add('PULL_LEVER');
    banned.add('GRASP_OBJECT');
    banned.add('PUNCH');
  }

  // 2. Locomotion & Leg Capability Analysis
  if (somatic.availableLegs < 2 || somatic.locomotionTier === 'LIMP') {
    banned.add('SPRINT');
    banned.add('LEAP');
  }

  if (somatic.locomotionTier === 'NEUROPATHY_SHUFFLE') {
    banned.add('SPRINT');
    banned.add('LEAP');
    banned.add('FAST_RETREAT');
  }

  if (somatic.availableLegs < 1 || somatic.locomotionTier === 'IMMOBILIZED') {
    banned.add('WALK');
    banned.add('SPRINT');
    banned.add('LEAP');
    banned.add('TACTICAL_REPOSITION');
  }

  // 3. Trauma Condition Overlays
  for (const condition of somatic.activeTrauma) {
    if (condition === 'sprained_wrist_right' || condition === 'dislocated_thumb') {
      banned.add('WIELD_TWO_HANDED');
      banned.add('HEAVY_DRAW');
    }
    if (condition === 'brachial_plexus_severed' || condition === 'paralyzed_left_arm') {
      banned.add('WIELD_TWO_HANDED');
      banned.add('CLIMB_ROPE');
    }
    if (condition === 'concussed_severe') {
      banned.add('AIMED_RANGED_ATTACK');
      banned.add('RAPID_PARRY');
    }
  }

  // 4. Exhaustion & Pain Penalties (Integer Bps Bounds)
  if (somatic.fatigueBps >= 7500 || somatic.painBps >= 8000) {
    banned.add('SPRINT');
    banned.add('HEAVY_ATTACK');
  }

  // 5. Integer Scalar Movement Budget Calculation
  let tierMultiplierBps = 10000; // 1.0x baseline
  if (somatic.locomotionTier === 'LIMP') {
    tierMultiplierBps = 15000; // 1.5x cost
  } else if (somatic.locomotionTier === 'NEUROPATHY_SHUFFLE') {
    tierMultiplierBps = 25000; // 2.5x cost
  } else if (somatic.locomotionTier === 'IMMOBILIZED') {
    tierMultiplierBps = 100000; // 10.0x cost
  }

  // Strict integer scaling using integer division
  const fatigueScaled = Math.floor((BASE_MOVEMENT_UNIT_COST * (10000 + somatic.fatigueBps)) / 10000);
  const movementBudgetCostUnits = Math.floor((fatigueScaled * tierMultiplierBps) / 10000);

  const bannedKineticVerbs = Object.freeze(Array.from(banned).sort());

  return Object.freeze({
    entityId: somatic.entityId,
    movementBudgetCostUnits,
    bannedKineticVerbs,
    canActuateTwoHandedVerbs: somatic.availableArms >= 2 && !banned.has('WIELD_TWO_HANDED'),
    canSprint: !banned.has('SPRINT'),
  });
};

export const validateHandCapacity = (
  availableArms: number,
  heldItems: readonly HeldItemDescriptor[],
): HandLoadEvaluation => {
  let totalOccupiedHands = 0;
  for (const item of heldItems) {
    totalOccupiedHands += item.occupiesHands;
  }

  const valid = totalOccupiedHands <= availableArms;
  return Object.freeze({
    valid,
    totalOccupiedHands,
    availableArms,
    error: valid
      ? undefined
      : `HAND_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED: Held items require ${totalOccupiedHands} hands but only ${availableArms} available`,
  });
};
```

#### 4. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/buz/budget-guard.buzz.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Traversal Budget Guard Buzzer
 * Enforces fail-closed halting when spatial traversal exceeds somatic energy budget.
 */

export interface BudgetGuardResult {
  readonly permitted: boolean;
  readonly traversalCostUnits: number;
  readonly allowedBudgetUnits: number;
  readonly marginUnits: number;
  readonly error?: string;
}

export const verifyTraversalBudget = (
  traversalCostUnits: number,
  allowedBudgetUnits: number,
): BudgetGuardResult => {
  if (!Number.isInteger(traversalCostUnits) || !Number.isInteger(allowedBudgetUnits)) {
    throw new Error('[BUDGET_GUARD_ERROR] Budget and traversal costs must be discrete integers');
  }

  const marginUnits = allowedBudgetUnits - traversalCostUnits;
  const permitted = marginUnits >= 0;

  return Object.freeze({
    permitted,
    traversalCostUnits,
    allowedBudgetUnits,
    marginUnits,
    error: permitted
      ? undefined
      : `BUDGET_EXCEEDED: Traversal cost of ${traversalCostUnits} exceeds somatic energy budget of ${allowedBudgetUnits}`,
  });
};
```

#### 7. `packages/300.engine/03.entity.unit/entity.reduce.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * 03.entity.unit - Entity Reducer Router
 */
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './entity.action.js';
import { EntityModel } from './entity.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './entity.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: EntityModel = new EntityModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State,
): EntityModel | Promise<EntityModel> {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_ENTITY:
      return Buzz.initEntity(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.UPDATE_ENTITY:
      return Buzz.updateEntity(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.DERIVE_SOMATIC_BUDGET: {
      try {
        const somatic = Buzz.extractSomaticState(act.bale?.dat);
        const receipt = Buzz.deriveSomaticBudget(somatic);
        if (act.bale?.slv) {
          act.bale.slv({
            entBit: {
              idx: 'derive-somatic-budget-success',
              val: receipt.movementBudgetCostUnits,
              dat: receipt as any,
            },
          });
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (act.bale?.slv) {
          act.bale.slv({
            entBit: {
              idx: 'derive-somatic-budget-fail',
              val: -1,
              dat: { error: err.message } as any,
            },
          });
        }
      }
      return model;
    }

    case Act.VALIDATE_HAND_CAPACITY: {
      const arms = (act.bale?.dat as any)?.availableArms ?? 2;
      const items = (act.bale?.dat as any)?.heldItems ?? [];
      const evaluation = Buzz.validateHandCapacity(arms, items);
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          entBit: {
            idx: evaluation.valid ? 'validate-hand-capacity-success' : 'validate-hand-capacity-fail',
            val: evaluation.valid ? 1 : 0,
            dat: evaluation as any,
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

#### 10. `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/location.reduce.ts` *(UPDATE)*

```typescript
/**
 * 10.location.unit - Location Reducer Router
 */
import clone from 'clone-deep';
import * as Act from './location.action.js';
import { LocationModel } from './location.model.js';
import * as Buzz from './location.buzzer.js';
import type State from '../99.core/state.js';

export function reducer(
  model: LocationModel = new LocationModel(),
  act: Act.Actions,
  state?: State,
): LocationModel | Promise<LocationModel> {
  switch (act.type) {
    case Act.INIT_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.initLocation(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.UPDATE_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.updateLocation(clone(model), act.bale, state);

    case Act.REGISTER_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.registerLocation(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.SET_PORTAL_LOCK:
      return Buzz.setPortalLockAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.VALIDATE_ADJACENCY:
      return Buzz.validateAdjacencyAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.CHECK_PORTAL_CROSSING:
      return Buzz.checkPortalCrossingAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.TRAVERSE_LOCATION:
      return Buzz.traverseLocationAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.AUDIT_TOPOLOGY:
      return Buzz.auditTopologyAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.BIND_SPATIAL_PORT:
      return Buzz.bindSpatialPortAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.EVALUATE_SPATIAL_PATH:
      return Buzz.evaluateSpatialPathAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.EVALUATE_SPATIAL_PERCEPTION:
      return Buzz.evaluateSpatialPerceptionAction(clone(model), act.bale as any, state);

    case Act.SANITIZE_PATH_QUERY: {
      const result = Buzz.sanitizePathQuery((act.bale?.dat as any)?.rawQuery);
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          locBit: {
            idx: result.ok ? 'sanitize-path-success' : 'sanitize-path-fail',
            val: result.ok ? 1 : 0,
            dat: { error: result.error, pathReceipt: result as any },
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    case Act.SANITIZE_PERCEPTION_QUERY: {
      const result = Buzz.sanitizePerceptionQuery((act.bale?.dat as any)?.rawQuery);
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          locBit: {
            idx: result.ok ? 'sanitize-perception-success' : 'sanitize-perception-fail',
            val: result.ok ? 1 : 0,
            dat: { error: result.error, perceptCandidate: result as any },
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    case Act.BUILD_SPATIAL_OVERLAY: {
      const snapshot = Buzz.buildSpatialOverlaySnapshot(
        model.portals,
        (act.bale?.dat as any)?.dynamicObstacles || [],
        (act.bale?.dat as any)?.costOverrides,
        (act.bale?.dat as any)?.acousticOverrides,
      );
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          locBit: {
            idx: 'build-spatial-overlay-success',
            src: snapshot.overlayHash,
            dat: { overlaySnapshot: snapshot },
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    case Act.VERIFY_TRAVERSAL_BUDGET: {
      const cost = (act.bale?.dat as any)?.traversalCostUnits ?? 0;
      const budget = (act.bale?.dat as any)?.allowedBudgetUnits ?? 0;
      const result = Buzz.verifyTraversalBudget(cost, budget);
      if (act.bale?.slv) {
        act.bale.slv({
          locBit: {
            idx: result.permitted ? 'verify-budget-success' : 'verify-budget-fail',
            val: result.permitted ? 1 : 0,
            dat: result as any,
          },
        });
      }
      return model;
    }

    default:
      return model;
  }
}
```

#### 11. `packages/300.engine/03.entity.unit/somatic-budget.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { extractSomaticState } from './buz/somatic-query.buzz.js';
import {
  BASE_MOVEMENT_UNIT_COST,
  deriveSomaticBudget,
  validateHandCapacity,
} from './buz/budget-calculator.buzz.js';
import { verifyTraversalBudget } from '../10.location.unit/buz/budget-guard.buzz.js';

describe('03.entity.unit & 10.location.unit - Somatic Condition Budget Translation Battery', () => {
  it('calculates uninjured baseline movement budget and unrestricted verbs', () => {
    const somatic = extractSomaticState({
      entityId: 'char_bog',
      availableArms: 2,
      availableLegs: 2,
      locomotionTier: 'UNRESTRICTED',
      fatigueBps: 0,
      painBps: 0,
      refusalStrainBps: 0,
      activeTrauma: [],
    });

    const receipt = deriveSomaticBudget(somatic);
    expect(receipt.movementBudgetCostUnits).toBe(BASE_MOVEMENT_UNIT_COST);
    expect(receipt.canSprint).toBe(true);
    expect(receipt.canActuateTwoHandedVerbs).toBe(true);
    expect(receipt.bannedKineticVerbs).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('scales budget and injects kinetic bans for limping locomotion and high fatigue', () => {
    const somatic = extractSomaticState({
      entityId: 'char_skulk',
      availableArms: 2,
      availableLegs: 1,
      locomotionTier: 'LIMP',
      fatigueBps: 5000, // 50.00% fatigue
      painBps: 2000,
      refusalStrainBps: 0,
      activeTrauma: [],
    });

    // base: 100 * (10000 + 5000) / 10000 = 150
    // tier: 150 * 15000 / 10000 = 225
    const receipt = deriveSomaticBudget(somatic);
    expect(receipt.movementBudgetCostUnits).toBe(225);
    expect(receipt.canSprint).toBe(false);
    expect(receipt.bannedKineticVerbs).toContain('SPRINT');
    expect(receipt.bannedKineticVerbs).toContain('LEAP');
  });

  it('prohibits two-handed actions and climbs on unilateral arm amputation', () => {
    const somatic = extractSomaticState({
      entityId: 'char_wart',
      availableArms: 1,
      availableLegs: 2,
      locomotionTier: 'UNRESTRICTED',
      fatigueBps: 0,
      painBps: 0,
      refusalStrainBps: 0,
      activeTrauma: ['brachial_plexus_severed'],
    });

    const receipt = deriveSomaticBudget(somatic);
    expect(receipt.canActuateTwoHandedVerbs).toBe(false);
    expect(receipt.bannedKineticVerbs).toContain('WIELD_TWO_HANDED');
    expect(receipt.bannedKineticVerbs).toContain('CLIMB_ROPE');
    expect(receipt.bannedKineticVerbs).toContain('DUAL_WIELD');
  });

  it('enforces Gillian integer basis-point bounds and float rejections', () => {
    expect(() =>
      extractSomaticState({
        entityId: 'char_broken',
        fatigueBps: 10001,
      }),
    ).toThrow('GILLIAN_BPS_BREACH');

    expect(() =>
      extractSomaticState({
        entityId: 'char_broken',
        fatigueBps: 25.5,
      }),
    ).toThrow('SOMATIC_INTEGER_BREACH');
  });

  it('validates hand capacity against active arm count', () => {
    const ok = validateHandCapacity(2, [
      { itemId: 'item_knife', occupiesHands: 1 },
      { itemId: 'item_torch', occupiesHands: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(ok.valid).toBe(true);
    expect(ok.totalOccupiedHands).toBe(2);

    const fail = validateHandCapacity(1, [
      { itemId: 'item_shield', occupiesHands: 1 },
      { itemId: 'item_spear', occupiesHands: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(fail.valid).toBe(false);
    expect(fail.error).toContain('HAND_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED');
  });

  it('budget guard fails closed when traversal cost exceeds somatic energy budget', () => {
    const allowed = verifyTraversalBudget(150, 200);
    expect(allowed.permitted).toBe(true);
    expect(allowed.marginUnits).toBe(50);

    const rejected = verifyTraversalBudget(250, 200);
    expect(rejected.permitted).toBe(false);
    expect(rejected.marginUnits).toBe(-50);
    expect(rejected.error).toContain('BUDGET_EXCEEDED');
  });
});
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

**User Story** Kael stared at the amber terminal phosphor of the simulation cockpit as the virtual chronometer ticked into cycle 104. In the dark, dripping cisterns beneath the culverts, the scavenger Wart crawled through the silt with one arm crushed beneath a rusted sluice gate. Under naive architectures, language models or pathfinding algorithms would treat Wart as a standard spatial pawn, allowing him to haul forty pounds of scrap iron and sprint up an iron ladder in two seconds.

Now, with Phase 3 active in `03.entity.unit` and `10.location.unit`, the simulation's biological conscience is unyielding. When an action requests Wart to vault over a barrier, the engine never sends his medical lore to the spatial oracle. Instead, the somatic decoupler queries his live ledger entry: one functional arm, a severe compound fracture, and fatigue sitting at 7,800 basis points. In less than two microseconds, the algebraic translator strips away his identity and outputs hard physical constraints: a scalar movement budget of 225 cost units, two-handed actuation prohibited, and the verb `VAULT` banned. When the path traversal attempts to spend 300 units, the budget guard drops a guillotine on the transaction before a single byte of disk is touched or a single token of dialogue is generated. Kael leaned back: the physical laws of the world were intact, immutable, and mathematically incorruptible.

**Strategic Goal** Supports the primary project objective of **Pre-Flight Somatic Capability Enforcement at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$**. By translating relational biological conditions into discrete scalar movement cost budgets (`movementBudgetCostUnits`), kinetic verb masks, and hand occupancy limits prior to spatial execution, the engine guarantees that physical impossibilities fail closed in host CPU memory before allocating expensive downstream media rendering, neural audio synthesis, or database persistence.

**Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Preserves absolute decoupling between biological state and spatial pathfinding. The spatial oracle (`000.goblin-spatial`) remains pure, stateless, and blind to character identities, medical trauma, and emotional grudges, evaluating only scalar cost budgets and geometry.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Enforcing the Gillian Arithmetic Doctrine using discrete integer arithmetic and basis points ($[0, 10000]\text{ bps}$) without floating-point math, while performing simultaneous feature roll-ups on motor limitations, trauma tags, and hand load constraints in sub-millisecond execution cycles.

**Interface**

- **Target Subsystems:** `000.series-sower-engine` (`packages/300.engine/03.entity.unit/` and `packages/300.engine/10.location.unit/`).
- **Interfacing Subsystems:**
  - `02.database.unit` / `entities_live`: Supplies live somatic condition rows (available limbs, fatigue, pain, trauma tags).
  - `000.goblin-spatial` (`SpatialPort`): Receives the sanitized `movementBudgetCostUnits` within `ScrubbedPathQuery` without receiving medical context.
  - `19.wounds.unit` / `18.trauma.unit`: Ingests the compiled `bannedKineticVerbs` for motor action verification.

**Interaction**

- **Blessed Terminal Cockpit Access:**
  1. The operator launches the terminal cockpit via `npm run dev` and selects `SIMULATION ENGINE` from the navigation switchboard.
  2. With the engine in `READY` status, the operator executes `[14] EXECUTE DETERMINISTIC` or `ADVANCE TICK`.
  3. When an actor initiates movement or item interaction, the somatic budget translator runs automatically in `03.entity.unit`.
- **Console Telemetry Output (`cns00`):**
  - Console pane `cns00` (columns 4–12) logs real-time 7-bit ASCII receipts:
    ```plaintext
    >> [SOMATIC:STATE] Ingested entity somatic profile: char_skulk
    >> [SOMATIC:BUDGET] Locomotion: LIMP | Fatigue: 5000 bps | Budget: 225 units
    >> [SOMATIC:VERBS] 2 kinetic verbs masked: [LEAP, SPRINT]
    >> [HANDS:SAT] Capacity check: 1/1 hands occupied [PASS]
    >> [BUDGET:GUARD] Traversal cost 150 <= allowed 225 [PERMITTED]
    ```

```typescript
import type { SpatialOverlaySnapshot } from './spatial-overlay.interface.js';

  readonly overlay: SpatialOverlaySnapshot;

}
```
